def discovered_law(positions, velocities, duration, **params):
    """The background particles source a softened radial field.
    Every particle accelerates along the local field gradient.
    The five probes respond to the field but contribute no source."""
    import numpy as np
    from scipy.integrate import solve_ivp
    pos = np.asarray(positions, dtype=float)
    vel = np.asarray(velocities, dtype=float)
    n = len(pos)
    nb = n - 5
    G = params.get("G", 0.2)
    beta = params.get("beta", 1.5)
    eps = params.get("eps", 0.5)
    y0 = np.concatenate((pos.ravel(), vel.ravel()))
    def rhs(t, y):
        r = y[:2*n].reshape(n, 2)
        v = y[2*n:].reshape(n, 2)
        d = r[None, :nb, :] - r[:, None, :]
        r2 = np.sum(d*d, axis=2) + eps*eps
        if nb:
            r2[np.arange(nb), np.arange(nb)] = np.inf
        a = G * np.sum(d / r2[:, :, None]**beta, axis=1)
        return np.concatenate((v.ravel(), a.ravel()))
    sol = solve_ivp(rhs, (0.0, duration), y0, method="RK45", rtol=1e-4, atol=1e-4)
    return sol.y[:2*n, -1].reshape(n, 2).tolist()

def fit_parameters():
    return {
        "G": {"init": 0.2, "bounds": [0.001, 2.0]},
        "beta": {"init": 1.5, "bounds": [0.5, 2.5]},
        "eps": {"init": 0.5, "bounds": [0.05, 3.0]}
    }
</run_mse_fit>

def discovered_law(positions, velocities, duration, **params):
    """The background particles source a softened radial field.
    Every particle accelerates along the local field gradient.
    The five probes respond to the field but contribute no source."""
    import numpy as np
    from scipy.integrate import solve_ivp
    pos = np.asarray(positions, dtype=float)
    vel = np.asarray(velocities, dtype=float)
    n = len(pos)
    nb = n - 5
    G = params.get("G", 0.2)
    beta = params.get("beta", 1.5)
    eps = params.get("eps", 0.5)
    y0 = np.concatenate((pos.ravel(), vel.ravel()))
    def rhs(t, y):
        r = y[:2*n].reshape(n, 2)
        v = y[2*n:].reshape(n, 2)
        d = r[None, :nb, :] - r[:, None, :]
        r2 = np.sum(d*d, axis=2) + eps*eps
        if nb:
            r2[np.arange(nb), np.arange(nb)] = np.inf
        a = G * np.sum(d / r2[:, :, None]**beta, axis=1)
        return np.concatenate((v.ravel(), a.ravel()))
    sol = solve_ivp(rhs, (0.0, duration), y0, method="RK45", rtol=1e-4, atol=1e-4)
    return sol.y[:2*n, -1].reshape(n, 2).tolist()

def fit_parameters():
    return {
        "G": {"init": 0.2, "bounds": [0.001, 2.0]},
        "beta": {"init": 1.5, "bounds": [0.5, 2.5]},
        "eps": {"init": 0.5, "bounds": [0.05, 3.0]}
    }

def discovered_law(positions, velocities, duration, **params):
    """Three background species source a softened radial field.
    Every particle accelerates along the combined local field gradient.
    The five neutral probes respond to the field but contribute no source."""
    import numpy as np
    from scipy.integrate import solve_ivp
    pos = np.asarray(positions, dtype=float)
    vel = np.asarray(velocities, dtype=float)
    n = len(pos)
    nb = n - 5
    w0 = params.get("w0", 0.5)
    w1 = params.get("w1", 0.5)
    w2 = params.get("w2", 0.5)
    beta = params.get("beta", 1.4)
    eps = params.get("eps", 0.6)
    weights = np.empty(nb)
    weights[:nb//3] = w0
    weights[nb//3:2*nb//3] = w1
    weights[2*nb//3:] = w2
    y0 = np.concatenate((pos.ravel(), vel.ravel()))
    def rhs(t, y):
        r = y[:2*n].reshape(n, 2)
        v = y[2*n:].reshape(n, 2)
        d = r[None, :nb, :] - r[:, None, :]
        r2 = np.sum(d*d, axis=2) + eps*eps
        r2[np.arange(nb), np.arange(nb)] = np.inf
        a = np.sum(weights[None, :, None] * d / r2[:, :, None]**beta, axis=1)
        return np.concatenate((v.ravel(), a.ravel()))
    sol = solve_ivp(rhs, (0.0, duration), y0, method="RK45", rtol=1e-4, atol=1e-4)
    return sol.y[:2*n, -1].reshape(n, 2).tolist()

def fit_parameters():
    return {
        "w0": {"init": 0.5, "bounds": [0.001, 3.0]},
        "w1": {"init": 0.5, "bounds": [0.001, 3.0]},
        "w2": {"init": 0.5, "bounds": [0.001, 3.0]},

[MSE Fit Input (law source)]
def discovered_law(positions, velocities, duration, **params):
    """Three background species source a softened radial field.
    Every particle accelerates along the combined local field gradient.
    The five neutral probes respond to the field but contribute no source."""
    import numpy as np
    from scipy.integrate import solve_ivp
    pos = np.asarray(positions, dtype=float)
    vel = np.asarray(velocities, dtype=float)
    n = len(pos)
    nb = n - 5
    w0 = params.get("w0", 0.5)
    w1 = params.get("w1", 0.5)
    w2 = params.get("w2", 0.5)
    beta = params.get("beta", 1.4)
    eps = params.get("eps", 0.6)
    weights = np.empty(nb)
    weights[:nb//3] = w0
    weights[nb//3:2*nb//3] = w1
    weights[2*nb//3:] = w2
    y0 = np.concatenate((pos.ravel(), vel.ravel()))
    def rhs(t, y):
        r = y[:2*n].reshape(n, 2)
        v = y[2*n:].reshape(n, 2)
        d = r[None, :nb, :] - r[:, None, :]
        r2 = np.sum(d*d, axis=2) + eps*eps
        r2[np.arange(nb), np.arange(nb)] = np.inf
        a = np.sum(weights[None, :, None] * d / r2[:, :, None]**beta, axis=1)
        return np.concatenate((v.ravel(), a.ravel()))
    sol = solve_ivp(rhs, (0.0, duration), y0, method="RK45", rtol=1e-4, atol=1e-4)
    return sol.y[:2*n, -1].reshape(n, 2).tolist()

def fit_parameters():
    return {
        "w0": {"init": 0.5, "bounds": [0.001, 3.0]},
        "w1": {"init": 0.5, "bounds": [0.001, 3.0]},
        "w2": {"init": 0.5, "bounds": [0.001, 3.0]},
        "beta": {"init": 1.4, "bounds": [0.5, 2.5]},
        "eps": {"init": 0.6, "bounds": [0.05, 3.0]}
    }

def discovered_law(positions, velocities, duration, **params):
    """Three contiguous background species source an attractive radial field.
    Every particle accelerates universally in the combined softened field.
    The five neutral probes respond to the field without sourcing it."""
    import numpy as np
    from scipy.integrate import solve_ivp
    pos = np.asarray(positions, dtype=float)
    vel = np.asarray(velocities, dtype=float)
    n = len(pos)
    nb = n - 5
    w0 = params.get("w0", 0.5)
    w1 = params.get("w1", 0.5)
    w2 = params.get("w2", 0.5)
    weights = np.empty(nb)
    weights[:nb//3] = w0
    weights[nb//3:2*nb//3] = w1
    weights[2*nb//3:] = w2
    y0 = np.concatenate((pos.ravel(), vel.ravel()))
    def rhs(t, y):
        r = y[:2*n].reshape(n, 2)
        v = y[2*n:].reshape(n, 2)
        d = r[None, :nb, :] - r[:, None, :]
        r2 = np.sum(d*d, axis=2) + 0.34
        r2[np.arange(nb), np.arange(nb)] = np.inf
        a = np.sum(weights[None, :, None] * d / r2[:, :, None]**1.4, axis=1)
        return np.concatenate((v.ravel(), a.ravel()))
    sol = solve_ivp(rhs, (0.0, duration), y0, method="RK45", rtol=2e-4, atol=2e-4)
    return sol.y[:2*n, -1].reshape(n, 2).tolist()

    return {

        "w1": {"init": 0.5, "bounds": [0.05, 1.5]},

[MSE Fit Input (law source)]
def discovered_law(positions, velocities, duration, **params):
    """Three contiguous background species source an attractive radial field.
    Every particle accelerates universally in the combined softened field.
    The five neutral probes respond to the field without sourcing it."""
    import numpy as np
    from scipy.integrate import solve_ivp
    pos = np.asarray(positions, dtype=float)
    vel = np.asarray(velocities, dtype=float)
    n = len(pos)
    nb = n - 5
    w0 = params.get("w0", 0.5)
    w1 = params.get("w1", 0.5)
    w2 = params.get("w2", 0.5)
    weights = np.empty(nb)
    weights[:nb//3] = w0
    weights[nb//3:2*nb//3] = w1
    weights[2*nb//3:] = w2
    y0 = np.concatenate((pos.ravel(), vel.ravel()))
    def rhs(t, y):
        r = y[:2*n].reshape(n, 2)
        v = y[2*n:].reshape(n, 2)
        d = r[None, :nb, :] - r[:, None, :]
        r2 = np.sum(d*d, axis=2) + 0.34
        r2[np.arange(nb), np.arange(nb)] = np.inf
        a = np.sum(weights[None, :, None] * d / r2[:, :, None]**1.4, axis=1)
        return np.concatenate((v.ravel(), a.ravel()))
    sol = solve_ivp(rhs, (0.0, duration), y0, method="RK45", rtol=2e-4, atol=2e-4)
    return sol.y[:2*n, -1].reshape(n, 2).tolist()

def fit_parameters():
    return {
        "w0": {"init": 0.5, "bounds": [0.05, 1.5]},
        "w1": {"init": 0.5, "bounds": [0.05, 1.5]},
        "w2": {"init": 0.5, "bounds": [0.05, 1.5]}
    }

def discovered_law(positions, velocities, duration, **params):
    """Three contiguous background species carry distinct signed source strengths.
    Every particle responds universally to their combined softened radial field.
    The five probes carry zero source strength while remaining field-sensitive."""
    import numpy as np
    from scipy.integrate import solve_ivp
    pos = np.asarray(positions, dtype=float)
    vel = np.asarray(velocities, dtype=float)
    n = len(pos)
    nb = n - 5
    weights = np.empty(nb)
    weights[:nb//3] = params.get("w0", -0.2)
    weights[nb//3:2*nb//3] = params.get("w1", 1.3)
    weights[2*nb//3:] = params.get("w2", -0.2)
    y0 = np.concatenate((pos.ravel(), vel.ravel()))
    def rhs(t, y):
        r = y[:2*n].reshape(n, 2)
        v = y[2*n:].reshape(n, 2)
        d = r[None, :nb, :] - r[:, None, :]
        r2 = np.sum(d*d, axis=2) + 0.34
        r2[np.arange(nb), np.arange(nb)] = np.inf
        a = np.sum(weights[None, :, None] * d / r2[:, :, None]**1.4, axis=1)
        return np.concatenate((v.ravel(), a.ravel()))
    sol = solve_ivp(rhs, (0.0, duration), y0, method="RK45", rtol=2e-4, atol=2e-4)
    return sol.y[:2*n, -1].reshape(n, 2).tolist()

def discovered_law(positions, velocities, duration, **params):
    """Three interleaved background species carry distinct signed source strengths.
    Every particle responds universally to their combined softened radial field.
    The five probes carry zero source strength while remaining field-sensitive."""
    import numpy as np
    from scipy.integrate import solve_ivp
    pos = np.asarray(positions, dtype=float)
    vel = np.asarray(velocities, dtype=float)
    n = len(pos)
    nb = n - 5
    species = np.arange(nb) % 3
    values = np.array([
        params.get("w0", -0.2),
        params.get("w1", 1.3),
        params.get("w2", -0.2)
    ])
    weights = values[species]
    y0 = np.concatenate((pos.ravel(), vel.ravel()))
    def rhs(t, y):
        r = y[:2*n].reshape(n, 2)
        v = y[2*n:].reshape(n, 2)
        d = r[None, :nb, :] - r[:, None, :]
        r2 = np.sum(d*d, axis=2) + 0.34
        r2[np.arange(nb), np.arange(nb)] = np.inf
        a = np.sum(weights[None, :, None] * d / r2[:, :, None]**1.4, axis=1)
        return np.concatenate((v.ravel(), a.ravel()))
    sol = solve_ivp(rhs, (0.0, duration), y0, method="RK45", rtol=2e-4, atol=2e-4)
    return sol.y[:2*n, -1].reshape(n, 2).tolist()

def discovered_law(positions, velocities, duration, **params):
    """Three contiguous species both source and respond to one signed radial field.
    A species charge scales its field contribution and its acceleration response.
    The five probes have unit response but exactly zero source charge."""
    import numpy as np
    from scipy.integrate import solve_ivp
    pos = np.asarray(positions, dtype=float)
    vel = np.asarray(velocities, dtype=float)
    n = len(pos)
    nb = n - 5
    qvals = np.array([
        params.get("q0", -0.12),
        params.get("q1", 1.74),
        params.get("q2", -0.33)
    ])
    q = np.empty(n)
    q[:nb//3] = qvals[0]
    q[nb//3:2*nb//3] = qvals[1]
    q[2*nb//3:nb] = qvals[2]
    q[nb:] = 1.0
    sources = q[:nb]
    y0 = np.concatenate((pos.ravel(), vel.ravel()))
    def rhs(t, y):
        r = y[:2*n].reshape(n, 2)
        v = y[2*n:].reshape(n, 2)
        d = r[None, :nb, :] - r[:, None, :]
        r2 = np.sum(d*d, axis=2) + 0.34
        r2[np.arange(nb), np.arange(nb)] = np.inf
        field = np.sum(sources[None, :, None] * d / r2[:, :, None]**1.4, axis=1)
        a = q[:, None] * field
        return np.concatenate((v.ravel(), a.ravel()))
    sol = solve_ivp(rhs, (0.0, duration), y0, method="RK45", rtol=2e-4, atol=2e-4)
    return sol.y[:2*n, -1].reshape(n, 2).tolist()

def fit_parameters():
    return {
        "q0": {"init": -0.12, "bounds": [-2.0, 2.0]},
        "q1": {"init": 1.74, "bounds": [-2.0, 2.0]},
        "q2": {"init": -0.33, "bounds": [-2.0, 2.0]}
    }
</run_mse_fit>

[MSE Fit Input (law source)]
def discovered_law(positions, velocities, duration, **params):
    """Three contiguous species both source and respond to one signed radial field.
    A species charge scales its field contribution and its acceleration response.
    The five probes have unit response but exactly zero source charge."""
    import numpy as np
    from scipy.integrate import solve_ivp
    pos = np.asarray(positions, dtype=float)
    vel = np.asarray(velocities, dtype=float)
    n = len(pos)
    nb = n - 5
    qvals = np.array([
        params.get("q0", -0.12),
        params.get("q1", 1.74),
        params.get("q2", -0.33)
    ])
    q = np.empty(n)
    q[:nb//3] = qvals[0]
    q[nb//3:2*nb//3] = qvals[1]
    q[2*nb//3:nb] = qvals[2]
    q[nb:] = 1.0
    sources = q[:nb]
    y0 = np.concatenate((pos.ravel(), vel.ravel()))
    def rhs(t, y):
        r = y[:2*n].reshape(n, 2)
        v = y[2*n:].reshape(n, 2)
        d = r[None, :nb, :] - r[:, None, :]
        r2 = np.sum(d*d, axis=2) + 0.34
        r2[np.arange(nb), np.arange(nb)] = np.inf
        field = np.sum(sources[None, :, None] * d / r2[:, :, None]**1.4, axis=1)
        a = q[:, None] * field
        return np.concatenate((v.ravel(), a.ravel()))
    sol = solve_ivp(rhs, (0.0, duration), y0, method="RK45", rtol=2e-4, atol=2e-4)
    return sol.y[:2*n, -1].reshape(n, 2).tolist()

def discovered_law(positions, velocities, duration, **params):
    """Only the central background species sources a softened attractive field.
    All three background species and the probes accelerate universally in that field.
    The probes and the two passive background species contribute no source."""
    import numpy as np
    from scipy.integrate import solve_ivp
    pos = np.asarray(positions, dtype=float)
    vel = np.asarray(velocities, dtype=float)
    n = len(pos)
    nb = n - 5
    weights = np.zeros(nb)
    weights[nb//3:2*nb//3] = params.get("G", 1.7)
    beta = params.get("beta", 1.4)
    eps = params.get("eps", 0.58)
    y0 = np.concatenate((pos.ravel(), vel.ravel()))
    def rhs(t, y):
        r = y[:2*n].reshape(n, 2)
        v = y[2*n:].reshape(n, 2)
        d = r[None, :nb, :] - r[:, None, :]
        r2 = np.sum(d*d, axis=2) + eps*eps
        r2[np.arange(nb), np.arange(nb)] = np.inf
        a = np.sum(weights[None, :, None] * d / r2[:, :, None]**beta, axis=1)
        return np.concatenate((v.ravel(), a.ravel()))
    sol = solve_ivp(rhs, (0.0, duration), y0, method="RK45", rtol=2e-4, atol=2e-4)
    return sol.y[:2*n, -1].reshape(n, 2).tolist()

def discovered_law(positions, velocities, duration, **params):
    """Three contiguous background species have source strengths in a one-to-two-to-three ratio.
    All particles accelerate universally in their combined softened radial field.
    The five probes respond to the field but contribute no source."""
    import numpy as np
    from scipy.integrate import solve_ivp
    pos = np.asarray(positions, dtype=float)
    vel = np.asarray(velocities, dtype=float)
    n = len(pos)
    nb = n - 5
    G = params.get("G", 0.25)
    beta = params.get("beta", 1.3)
    eps = params.get("eps", 0.6)
    weights = np.empty(nb)
    weights[:nb//3] = G
    weights[nb//3:2*nb//3] = 2.0*G
    weights[2*nb//3:] = 3.0*G
    y0 = np.concatenate((pos.ravel(), vel.ravel()))
    def rhs(t, y):
        r = y[:2*n].reshape(n, 2)
        v = y[2*n:].reshape(n, 2)
        d = r[None, :nb, :] - r[:, None, :]
        r2 = np.sum(d*d, axis=2) + eps*eps
        r2[np.arange(nb), np.arange(nb)] = np.inf
        a = np.sum(weights[None, :, None] * d / r2[:, :, None]**beta, axis=1)
        return np.concatenate((v.ravel(), a.ravel()))
    sol = solve_ivp(rhs, (0.0, duration), y0, method="RK45", rtol=2e-4, atol=2e-4)
    return sol.y[:2*n, -1].reshape(n, 2).tolist()

def fit_parameters():
    return {
        "G": {"init": 0.25, "bounds": [0.01, 1.5]},
        "beta": {"init": 1.3, "bounds": [0.5, 2.5]},
        "eps": {"init": 0.6, "bounds": [0.05, 3.0]}
    }

def discovered_law(positions, velocities, duration, **params):
    """The middle of three contiguous species sources three times the radial field of the outer species.
    Every particle accelerates universally in the combined softened field.
    The five probes respond to the field but contribute no source."""
    import numpy as np
    from scipy.integrate import solve_ivp
    pos = np.asarray(positions, dtype=float)
    vel = np.asarray(velocities, dtype=float)
    n = len(pos)
    nb = n - 5
    G = params.get("G", 0.4)
    beta = params.get("beta", 1.3)
    eps = params.get("eps", 0.6)
    weights = np.empty(nb)
    weights[:nb//3] = G
    weights[nb//3:2*nb//3] = 3.0*G
    weights[2*nb//3:] = G
    y0 = np.concatenate((pos.ravel(), vel.ravel()))
    def rhs(t, y):
        r = y[:2*n].reshape(n, 2)
        v = y[2*n:].reshape(n, 2)
        d = r[None, :nb, :] - r[:, None, :]
        r2 = np.sum(d*d, axis=2) + eps*eps
        r2[np.arange(nb), np.arange(nb)] = np.inf
        a = np.sum(weights[None, :, None] * d / r2[:, :, None]**beta, axis=1)
        return np.concatenate((v.ravel(), a.ravel()))
    sol = solve_ivp(rhs, (0.0, duration), y0, method="RK45", rtol=2e-4, atol=2e-4)
    return sol.y[:2*n, -1].reshape(n, 2).tolist()

def discovered_law(positions, velocities, duration, **params):
    """The middle of three contiguous species sources three times the radial field of the outer species.
    Every particle accelerates universally in the combined softened field.
    The five probes respond to the field but contribute no source."""
    import numpy as np
    from scipy.integrate import solve_ivp
    pos = np.asarray(positions, dtype=float)
    vel = np.asarray(velocities, dtype=float)
    n = len(pos)
    nb = n - 5
    G = params.get("G", 0.4)
    beta = params.get("beta", 1.3)
    eps = params.get("eps", 0.6)
    weights = np.empty(nb)
    weights[:nb//3] = G
    weights[nb//3:2*nb//3] = 3.0*G
    weights[2*nb//3:] = G
    y0 = np.concatenate((pos.ravel(), vel.ravel()))
    def rhs(t, y):
        r = y[:2*n].reshape(n, 2)
        v = y[2*n:].reshape(n, 2)
        d = r[None, :nb, :] - r[:, None, :]
        r2 = np.sum(d*d, axis=2) + eps*eps
        r2[np.arange(nb), np.arange(nb)] = np.inf
        a = np.sum(weights[None, :, None] * d / r2[:, :, None]**beta, axis=1)
        return np.concatenate((v.ravel(), a.ravel()))
    sol = solve_ivp(rhs, (0.0, duration), y0, method="RK45", rtol=2e-4, atol=2e-4)
    return sol.y[:2*n, -1].reshape(n, 2).tolist()

def fit_parameters():
    return {
        "G": {"init": 0.4, "bounds": [0.01, 1.5]},
        "beta": {"init": 1.3, "bounds": [0.5, 2.5]},
        "eps": {"init": 0.6, "bounds": [0.05, 3.0]}
    }

def discovered_law(positions, velocities, duration, **params):
    """The central background species sources an exponentially screened radial field.
    All background particles and probes accelerate along its softened field gradient.
    The two outer species and the five probes respond without contributing sources."""
    import numpy as np
    from scipy.integrate import solve_ivp
    pos = np.asarray(positions, dtype=float)
    vel = np.asarray(velocities, dtype=float)
    n = len(pos)
    nb = n - 5
    G = params.get("G", 1.0)
    beta = params.get("beta", 1.0)
    lam = params.get("lam", 10.0)
    eps = params.get("eps", 0.6)
    weights = np.zeros(nb)
    weights[nb//3:2*nb//3] = G
    y0 = np.concatenate((pos.ravel(), vel.ravel()))
    def rhs(t, y):
        r = y[:2*n].reshape(n, 2)
        v = y[2*n:].reshape(n, 2)
        d = r[None, :nb, :] - r[:, None, :]
        r2 = np.sum(d*d, axis=2) + eps*eps
        r2[np.arange(nb), np.arange(nb)] = np.inf
        dist = np.sqrt(r2)
        kernel = np.exp(-dist/lam) / r2**beta
        a = np.sum(weights[None, :, None] * d * kernel[:, :, None], axis=1)
        return np.concatenate((v.ravel(), a.ravel()))
    sol = solve_ivp(rhs, (0.0, duration), y0, method="RK45", rtol=2e-4, atol=2e-4)
    return sol.y[:2*n, -1].reshape(n, 2).tolist()

def fit_parameters():
    return {
        "G": {"init": 1.0, "bounds": [0.05, 5.0]},
        "beta": {"init": 1.0, "bounds": [0.3, 2.0]},
        "lam": {"init": 10.0, "bounds": [1.0, 50.0]},
        "eps": {"init": 0.6, "bounds": [0.05, 4.0]}
    }

def discovered_law(positions, velocities, duration, **params):
    """The central background species sources a smooth Gaussian radial field.
    Every particle accelerates toward source particles with a finite interaction range.
    The outer background species and five probes respond but do not source the field."""
    import numpy as np
    from scipy.integrate import solve_ivp
    pos = np.asarray(positions, dtype=float)
    vel = np.asarray(velocities, dtype=float)
    n = len(pos)
    nb = n - 5
    G = params.get("G", 0.02)
    length = params.get("length", 6.0)
    weights = np.zeros(nb)
    weights[nb//3:2*nb//3] = G
    y0 = np.concatenate((pos.ravel(), vel.ravel()))
    def rhs(t, y):
        r = y[:2*n].reshape(n, 2)
        v = y[2*n:].reshape(n, 2)
        d = r[None, :nb, :] - r[:, None, :]
        r2 = np.sum(d*d, axis=2)
        kernel = np.exp(-r2/(2.0*length*length))
        kernel[np.arange(nb), np.arange(nb)] = 0.0
        a = np.sum(weights[None, :, None] * d * kernel[:, :, None], axis=1)
        return np.concatenate((v.ravel(), a.ravel()))
    sol = solve_ivp(rhs, (0.0, duration), y0, method="RK45", rtol=2e-4, atol=2e-4)
    return sol.y[:2*n, -1].reshape(n, 2).tolist()

def fit_parameters():
    return {
        "G": {"init": 0.02, "bounds": [0.0001, 1.0]},

[MSE Fit Input (law source)]
def discovered_law(positions, velocities, duration, **params):
    """The central background species sources a smooth Gaussian radial field.
    Every particle accelerates toward source particles with a finite interaction range.
    The outer background species and five probes respond but do not source the field."""
    import numpy as np
    from scipy.integrate import solve_ivp
    pos = np.asarray(positions, dtype=float)
    vel = np.asarray(velocities, dtype=float)
    n = len(pos)
    nb = n - 5
    G = params.get("G", 0.02)
    length = params.get("length", 6.0)
    weights = np.zeros(nb)
    weights[nb//3:2*nb//3] = G
    y0 = np.concatenate((pos.ravel(), vel.ravel()))
    def rhs(t, y):
        r = y[:2*n].reshape(n, 2)
        v = y[2*n:].reshape(n, 2)
        d = r[None, :nb, :] - r[:, None, :]
        r2 = np.sum(d*d, axis=2)
        kernel = np.exp(-r2/(2.0*length*length))
        kernel[np.arange(nb), np.arange(nb)] = 0.0
        a = np.sum(weights[None, :, None] * d * kernel[:, :, None], axis=1)
        return np.concatenate((v.ravel(), a.ravel()))
    sol = solve_ivp(rhs, (0.0, duration), y0, method="RK45", rtol=2e-4, atol=2e-4)
    return sol.y[:2*n, -1].reshape(n, 2).tolist()

def fit_parameters():
    return {
        "G": {"init": 0.02, "bounds": [0.0001, 1.0]},
        "length": {"init": 6.0, "bounds": [0.5, 30.0]}
    }

    """Three contiguous species have distinct source strengths for one Gaussian field.

    The five probes carry no source strength while following the field."""
    import numpy as np

    vel = np.asarray(velocities, dtype=float)

        params.get("w1", 0.045),
        params.get("w2", 0.0)
    ])
    length = params.get("length", 4.5)
    weights = np.empty(nb)
    weights[:nb//3] = values[0]

    weights[2*nb//3:] = values[2]

        v = y[2*n:].reshape(n, 2)

def discovered_law(positions, velocities, duration, **params):
    """Three particle species source a shared Gaussian-smoothed potential with different signed strengths.
    Every particle accelerates along the gradient of this nonlocal field.
    The five neutral probes respond to the field but contribute no source."""
    import numpy as np
    from scipy.integrate import solve_ivp
    pos = np.asarray(positions, dtype=float)
    vel = np.asarray(velocities, dtype=float)
    n = len(pos)
    nb = n - 5
    w0 = params.get("w0", 0.0051177998733832365)
    w1 = params.get("w1", 0.03916211781883171)
    w2 = params.get("w2", -0.014143983323707568)
    length = params.get("length", 5.076726142777977)
    shape = params.get("shape", 2.0)
    weights = np.empty(nb)
    weights[:nb // 3] = w0
    weights[nb // 3:2 * nb // 3] = w1
    weights[2 * nb // 3:] = w2
    y0 = np.concatenate((pos.ravel(), vel.ravel()))

    def rhs(t, y):
        r = y[:2 * n].reshape(n, 2)
        v = y[2 * n:].reshape(n, 2)
        displacement = r[None, :nb, :] - r[:, None, :]
        radius2 = np.sum(displacement * displacement, axis=2)
        scaled = np.maximum(radius2 / (length * length), 0.0)
        kernel = np.exp(-0.5 * scaled ** (0.5 * shape))
        kernel[np.arange(nb), np.arange(nb)] = 0.0
        acceleration = np.sum(
            weights[None, :, None] * displacement * kernel[:, :, None],
            axis=1
        )
        return np.concatenate((v.ravel(), acceleration.ravel()))

    solution = solve_ivp(
        rhs,
        (0.0, duration),
        y0,
        method="RK45",
        rtol=2e-4,
        atol=2e-4
    )
    return solution.y[:2 * n, -1].reshape(n, 2).tolist()

  Agent explanation   : The background consists of three contiguous species whose signed source strengths generate a shared, finite-range field equivalent to the gradient of a Gaussian-smoothed potential. Every particle responds universally to that field, while the final five neutral probes have zero source strength and therefore measure the evolving background without perturbing it.
  Optimal explanation : Thirty-five particles interact through a static Laplacian field, ∇²φ = source, with acceleration -∇φ. The 30 background particles split into three hidden species: particles 0–9 with source coupling +1, particles 10–19 with +3 (strong attractors), and particles 20–29 with -2 (repulsive — they source a field that pushes other particles away rather than pulling them in). Particles 30–34 are neutral probes with zero coupling, feeling forces without sourcing the field.
  Explanation score   : 0.40  (raw 4/10)
  Judge reasoning     :
The student correctly captures the three contiguous source species, the universal force response, and the five neutral probes with zero source coupling — solid structural features. However, the field characterization is wrong: it describes a "finite-range" "Gaussian-smoothed potential" rather than the static long-range Laplacian (∇²φ = source), and "evolving background" muddies the static character. It also never explicitly identifies a repulsive (negative) species or provides the +1 : +3 : −2 coupling ratios, only vaguely gesturing at "signed source strengths."
